# 分镜 H3 视频提示词 · 强化版

## 本套强化采用的运镜与视频约束规则

按集读取 storyboard 的 E##-##/prompt.md，仅强化各镜头的 integrated_multimodal_description，不改动 `[Shot k]` 分段、Picture 挂图与钉点、overall_soundscape、non_diegetic_music ≥ 全部保留原样，镜头动作/叙事/时长不变。整体保持英文。两套强化维度：① 运镜：每镜只给一种平缓电影级镜头运动（慢推 slow push in / 缓横移 slow lateral track / 缓环绕 slow orbit / 轻拉出 gentle dolly-out / 轻呼吸手持 static breathing handheld），禁止一段里堆叠多种剧变运镜，用词升级为专业表述（推进/斯德 rack、环绕、跟移、升降、拉）。② 稳定性：动作强度 low~moderate，主体外形全程不变形、不换脸、不跳帧，只保留发丝/水流/衣袂/尘埃等局部自然微动。逐镜把「保持主体外形不变」语义自然地融进画面/运镜描述里。

---

# E01

### E01-01
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, a static shot frames a circle of jostling monkeys by a pool before a straight white waterfall in bright noon light, none daring to approach the curtain of water.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, a static breathing handheld holds a circle of jostling monkeys by a pool before a straight white waterfall in bright noon light, subtle handheld sway only; none daring to approach the curtain of water. Keep every monkey's outline locked and stable — only fur, dust and the spray drift naturally; no morphing, no distortion.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:02.500, a slow push in toward the young monkey who elbows through the crowd to the poolside, plants his feet, squares up and stares up at the hanging waterfall with bright eyes.
  - **强化**：At 00:02.500, a single slow push in (smooth rack focus onto the face) follows the young monkey who elbows through the crowd to the poolside, plants his feet, squares up and stares up at the hanging waterfall with bright eyes. Motion stays low-to-moderate, his form held steady and proportionate throughout, only whiskers and fur flicking.
- **Shot 3** 原文：At 00:05.000, static shot, a fixed shot holds the young monkey bracing a branch against the ground, sucking in a breath, then kicking off the ground and leaping straight into the falling water before the watching monkeys.
  - **强化**：At 00:05.000, static breathing handheld holds the young monkey bracing a branch against the ground, sucking in a breath, then kicking off and leaping straight into the falling water. Restrain the leap to a moderate arc so his body stays stable and undistorted mid-air, water flinging naturally around him.

### E01-02
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, a static shot captures the white water swallowing the young monkey whole, spray bursting high as the monkeys on the bank all flinch back two steps.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, a static shot with micro drift captures the white water swallowing the young monkey whole, spray bursting high as the monkeys on the bank flinch back two steps. Bank monkeys keep their forms locked — only spray, foam and flinching displacement move; no warp, no ghosting.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:02.500, static shot, a fixed close-to-medium look at the young monkey who pokes out half a wet face from behind the water curtain and waves hard toward the bank, water dripping from his fur.
  - **强化**：At 00:02.500, gentle slow push in toward a fixed close-to-medium look as the young monkey pokes out half a wet face from behind the water curtain and waves hard toward the bank, water dripping from his fur. His facial features remain stable and continuous — only water beads and fur run off naturally.
- **Shot 3** 原文：At 00:07.500, static shot, a static close-up on the wet young monkey as he shouts across the water at the top of his lungs.
  - **强化**：At 00:07.500, static close-up with a faint slow push in on the wet young monkey as he shouts across the water at the top of his lungs. Keep the close-up form rigid and coherent — lips and brow animate while the face never melts or reshapes.

### E01-03
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, static shot, a fixed medium shot watches the young monkey duck back behind the hanging water curtain after his shout, the veil of water falling closed as he vanishes.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, static breathing handheld fixed medium shot watches the young monkey duck back behind the hanging water curtain after his shout, the veil of water falling closed as he vanishes. Water curtain parts and seals smoothly; his body reads as one continuous form behind it.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:02.500, static shot, a wide fixed shot in which the leading wild monkey first bravely jumps down with a splash and the rest follow one after another, leaping into the water curtain in a chain of spray.
  - **强化**：At 00:02.500, steady slow lateral track across a wide fixed field as the leading wild monkey jumps down with a splash and the rest follow one after another in a chain of spray. Each monkey's silhouette stays stable and non-deforming mid-leap; only spray and foam ripple.
- **Shot 3** 原文：At 00:05.000, a wide static shot holds the young monkey crossing the iron-slab bridge, jumping onto the great natural stone seat at the center of the cave, sitting cross-legged and surveying the whole cavern.
  - **强化**：At 00:05.000, a wide shot with a slow push in holds the young monkey crossing the iron-slab bridge, jumping onto the great natural stone seat at the center of the cave, sitting cross-legged and surveying the whole cavern. His posture locks into a stable sitting frame; surrounding rock and water only breathe subtly.

### E01-04
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, a wide static shot shows the monkeys who entered last dropping into a kneeling line around the base of the stone seat, their chorus ringing through the cavern.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, a wide static shot with a gentle vertical drift (slow tilt) shows the monkeys who entered last dropping into a kneeling line around the base of the stone seat, their chorus ringing through the cavern. Crowd keeps coherent forms — only shoulders and ears twitch as they bow.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:02.400, static shot, a medium fixed shot looks down at the kneeling monkeys who look up at the young monkey, who nods slowly and raises a hand in a gentle gesture.
  - **强化**：At 00:02.400, static breathing handheld medium fixed shot looks down at the kneeling monkeys who look up at the young monkey, who nods slowly and raises a hand. All head turns are small and stable; faces stay unchanged, only fur and whiskers sway.
- **Shot 3** 原文：At 00:04.900, static shot, a static close-up on the young monkey who lifts his hand and smiles warmly while he speaks.
  - **强化**：At 00:04.900, static close-up with a slow push in on the young monkey who lifts his hand and smiles warmly. The smile animates only the mouth corners and eyes; the skull and features hold rigidly stable.

### E01-05
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, static shot, a static close-up frames the leading wild monkey who lifts his face and jeers loudly and joyfully, the crowd behind him chattering into a din.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, static breathing handheld close-up frames the leading wild monkey who lifts his face and jeers loudly, the crowd behind chattering into a din. Intensive magnitude low: only mouth, eyes and fur move; no facial warp, no extra frames flickering.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:04.400, static shot, a static close-up on the young monkey who slaps his knee and takes up the crown lightly, his brows lifting in open pride as he speaks.
  - **强化**：At 00:04.400, static close-up with slow push in on the young monkey who slaps his knee and takes up the crown lightly, brows lifting in open pride. His form stays imported-locked; motion confined to the gesture and expression.
- **Shot 3** 原文：At 00:07.300, static shot, a wide fixed shot in which the monkeys burst into celebration, jumping and shouting around the stone seat, water flicking off their heads in every direction.
  - **强化**：At 00:07.300, gentle slow orbit around a wide fixed field as the monkeys burst into celebration, jumping and shouting around the stone seat, water flicking off their heads. Movement kept moderate so bouncing bodies stay cohesive and non-distorted.

### E01-06
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, a medium static shot shows a small monkey before the seat beaming up at the young monkey, admiration plain on his face.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, a medium shot with a soft slow push in shows a small monkey before the seat beaming up at the young monkey, admiration plain on his face. Only the grin and bright eyes animate; head stays stable.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:03.600, static shot, a static close-up on the young monkey who pats his knee and speaks to the crowd with grave, settled tone.
  - **强化**：At 00:03.600, static breathing handheld close-up on the young monkey who pats his knee and speaks with grave, settled tone. Speech stays natural lip-sync; face remains rigidly proportionate.
- **Shot 3** 原文：At 00:08.000, static shot, a medium fixed shot in which the monkeys answer in crisp unison around the stone seat.
  - **强化**：At 00:08.000, gentle lateral track over a medium fixed frame as the monkeys answer in crisp unison around the stone seat. Each head turn is small and simultaneous; forms stay steady.

### E01-07
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, static shot, a static close shot frames a small monkey walking up to the stone seat carrying a heaping little pile of wild fruit and offering it up respectfully.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, static breathing handheld close shot frames a small monkey walking up to the stone seat carrying wild fruit and offering it up respectfully. His gait is slow and steady; the pile is kept stable without morphing.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:02.500, static shot, a static close-up on the young monkey who takes the fruit and, instead of eating, first gestures out to everyone around him.
  - **强化**：At 00:02.500, static close-up with slow push in on the young monkey who takes the fruit and first gestures out to everyone. The overreach gesture is gentle; face and fur hold firm.
- **Shot 3** 原文：At 00:06.500, static shot, a medium fixed shot in which the young monkey breaks the fruit apart, hands pieces to the old and young monkeys beside him, and only then takes his own bite.
  - **强化**：At 00:06.500, gentle dolly-back to medium as the young monkey breaks the fruit apart, hands pieces to the old and young monkeys, then takes his own bite. Motion low-to-moderate; hands and faces never distort.

### E01-08
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, a medium static shot shows a small monkey leading the young monkey to the corner of the cave and pointing at a row of stacked stone stoves and pans for him to see.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, a medium shot with slow lateral track shows a small monkey leading the young monkey to the corner of the cave and pointing at stacked stone stoves and pans. Tracking is even; focus holds on faces, forms stable.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:05.000, static shot, a medium fixed shot on the small monkey, who points at the stove with delight and surprise.
  - **强化**：At 00:05.000, static breathing handheld medium fixed shot on the small monkey who points at the stove with delight. Only his arm and expression move; figure stays rigid.
- **Shot 3** 原文：At 00:08.600, static shot, a static close-up in which the young monkey crouches, runs a hand over the soot-blackened stove rim, then lifts his head and takes in the whole cavern.
  - **强化**：At 00:08.600, static close-up with slow push in as the young monkey crouches, runs a hand over the soot-blackened stove rim, then lifts his head. Head lift is a clean rack focus; silhouette held stable.

### E01-09
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, static shot, a static close-up on the young monkey whose voice drops on the last phrase as if speaking to himself, a touch of a smile fading.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, static breathing handheld close-up on the young monkey whose voice drops on the last phrase, a smile fading. Subtle change only in brow and mouth; features never blend between frames.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:03.800, a wide static shot sweeps the crowd as his words land in the celebration; the laughing monkeys all fall silent for a beat.
  - **强化**：At 00:03.800, a slow lateral track sweeps the crowd as his words land; the laughing monkeys fall silent for a beat. Sway is paced; each monkey kept as one consistent form.
- **Shot 3** 原文：At 00:06.300, static shot, a static close-up on an old monkey by the corner wall, leaning on a cane, who slowly shakes his head and lets out a long, worn-out sigh.
  - **强化**：At 00:06.300, static close-up with slow push in on an old monkey leaning on a cane who slowly shakes his head with a long sigh. The slow shake is small and controlled; wrinkles and fur read natively.

### E01-10
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, a static shot holds on the two of them as the old monkey finishes with a sigh, and the monkey king blinks, tilts his head and asks the question back.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, a static breathing handheld medium holds on the two; the old monkey finishes with a sigh and the king blinks, tilts his head and asks back. Both figures stay locked; only blink, tilt and breath animate.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:04.400, static shot, a static close-up on the old monkey who now speaks very slowly, dwelling word by word on the sickness and the buried earth that no one escapes.
  - **强化**：At 00:04.400, static close-up with slow push in on the old monkey speaking very slowly on sickness and the buried earth. Slow deliberate speech; face shape held invariant.
- **Shot 3** 原文：At 00:09.100, static shot, a static medium shot in which the young monkey's face darkens at the words, his neck stiffening as he falls quiet, eyes going blank.
  - **强化**：At 00:09.100, gentle dolly-out to medium as the young monkey's face darkens, neck stiffening as he falls quiet. Change is purely in expression; the form stays rigid and still.

### E01-11
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, static shot, a static close-up on the young monkey whose voice turns hoarse as he asks, neck stiff, eyes urgent.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, static breathing handheld close-up on the young monkey whose voice turns hoarse as he asks, neck stiff, eyes urgent. Urgency animates only pupils and brow; face never warps.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:03.300, static shot, a static close-up on the old monkey who shakes his head, speaking with grave weight.
  - **强化**：At 00:03.300, static close-up with slow push in on the old monkey who shakes his head with grave weight. Head shake kept small; fur and beard sway naturally.
- **Shot 3** 原文：At 00:07.100, static shot, a static close-up on the young monkey who flinches as if pricked by a needle, falling silent for a long moment.
  - **强化**：At 00:07.100, static close-up on the young monkey who flinches as if pricked by a needle, then falls silent. Flinch is a single tight jolt, not a reshape; stillness afterward is clean.

  - **强化**：At 00:09.600, static close-up with micro push in as the young monkey clenches his fist and speaks with a rebellious edge. Fist and eyes carry the tension; form stays unchanged.

### E01-13
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, static shot, a static close-up on the young monkey whose eyes grow brighter as he speaks with certainty, resolved to cross the seas and climb the mountains.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, static close-up with slow push in on the young monkey whose eyes grow brighter as he speaks. Brightness rises in eyes only; face stays rigid.

  - **强化**：At 00:07.800, a steady slow lateral track follows the young monkey springing to his feet and pacing the cavern three times, faster each lap. Speed builds only moderately so movement stays fluid and his form non-deforming.

### E01-14
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, static shot, a static medium shot shows the young monkey stopping at the mouth of the cave, facing the hanging water curtain, his brow slowly knitting.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, static breathing handheld medium shows the young monkey stopping at the cave mouth facing the water curtain, brow slowly knitting. Knit is a slow brow-only change; body still.

  - **强化**：At 00:05.000, static close-up with slow push in as the young monkey lifts his head to the starry sky, brow tightening. Head lift is a steady tilt; face held stable.

  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, static breathing handheld close-up on the young monkey gazing at the stars, voice turning low and heavy. Gaze is fixed; only eyes glint, form unmoving.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:03.800, static shot, a medium fixed shot in which he reaches up with one hand into the moonlight, the palm closing on nothing, then slowly lowering.
  - **强化**：At 00:03.800, slow push in over a medium frame as he reaches up with one hand into the moonlight, palm closing on nothing, then slowly lowering. The hand rise/fall is a single smooth gesture; no shimmer warp.

  - **强化**：At 00:06.300, static close-up with micro push in as the young monkey speaks word by word like a vow, heavy and slow. Heavy cadence; face locked and stable.

### E01-16
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, static shot, a static medium shot shows the young monkey gazing at the stars, chest rising and falling as if a great resolve has settled.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, a medium shot with slow push in shows the young monkey gazing at the stars, chest rising and falling as a great resolve settles. Only breathing moves the chest; the rest stays still.

  - **强化**：At 00:02.500, slow lateral track over a medium frame on a small monkey half-awake, urging drowsily. Drowsy speech is gentle; he sways very slightly, form stable.

  - **强化**：At 00:05.600, static breathing handheld close-up on the young monkey answering without turning his head, urgency plain. Answer without a back; only jaw and eyes move.

### E01-17
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, a wide static shot shows the young monkey springing up abruptly, sweeping a last look over the sleeping monkeys as if saying goodbye to them.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, a wide static shot with a slow lateral pan shows the young monkey springing up abruptly, sweeping a last look over the sleeping monkeys. The sudden rise is a single clean motion; his figure stays rigid.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:02.500, static shot, a static close-up on his hand as he grabs a wild fruit and tucks it into his clothes as road rations for the coming days.
  - **强化**：At 00:02.500, slow push in on a static close-up of his hand as he grabs a wild fruit and tucks it into his clothes. Fingers move precisely; palm and fruit keep consistent scale.
- **Shot 3** 原文：At 00:05.000, a tracking shot follows the young monkey as he turns, runs out of the cave without looking back, and vanishes into the night.
  - **强化**：At 00:05.000, a smooth lateral tracking shot follows him as he turns, runs out of the cave without looking back, vanishing into the night. Tracking pace is even; running stride stays natural, no frame-skipping.
- **Shot 4** 原文：At 00:07.500, static shot, a wide fixed shot in which he reaches the foot of the hill at a run, stops at a fork in the road, catches his breath, and scans his bearings.
  - **强化**：At 00:07.500, static breathing handheld wide shot as he reaches the foot of the hill at a run, stops at a fork, catches his breath and scans his bearings. Breath is a light chest rise; all poses held steady.

### E01-18
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, static shot, a static close-up on the young monkey squinting against the night wind, saying the line with full spirit, eyes bright.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, static close-up with slow push in on the young monkey squinting against the night wind, saying the line with full spirit. Wind animates only fur and garment edge; face and eyes stay crisp.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:05.100, pull out, as the pull-out widens, the young monkey turns his head, strides off down the farther mountain road, his figure swallowed little by little into the night.
  - **强化**：At 00:05.100, one gentle dolly-out widens as he turns his head, strides off down the farther road, his figure swallowed into the night. Dolly speed is smooth; figure shrinks by real distance, never by distortion.

# E02

### E02-01
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, a tracking shot follows the running monkey king from behind as he trots along a dusk dirt road carrying a bulging bundle, withered yellow grass flattening in the wind and cart ruts sliding away behind his feet.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, a smooth lateral tracking shot follows the running monkey king from behind as he trots along a dusk dirt road carrying a bulging bundle, withered grass flattening in the wind and cart ruts sliding away. Tracking stays even so his stride is stable and non-deforming; only grass, dust and bundle cloth flutter.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:05.000, the tracking shot continues without a break as he trips over a clump of grass and falls hard, his bundle flying off his shoulder; he scrambles up, dusts the dirt off, snatches the bundle back and stumbles onward.
  - **强化**：At 00:05.000, the lateral tracking shot continues without a break as he trips, falls hard, bundle flying off his shoulder, then scrambles up, dusts off, snatches the bundle and stumbles onward. Keep the tumble to moderate so his body stays one connected mass; dust rises as the only extra motion.
- **Shot 3** 原文：At 00:10.000, static shot, a static close-up holds the young monkey panting as he speaks toward the distant peak.
  - **强化**：At 00:10.000, static breathing handheld close-up holds the young monkey panting as he speaks toward the distant peak. Panting moves only his chest and breath; face and fur stay fixed and clear.

### E02-02
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, a static shot holds the young monkey before a weathered stone pavilion, craning up at a faded banner whose ink has worn blurry.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, a static shot with slow push in holds the young monkey before a weathered stone pavilion, craning up at a faded banner. Cranial tilt is slow and clean; the banner flaps while his form stays rigid.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:02.500, static shot, a static close-up on the young monkey squinting and reading the banner aloud, trailing off as the second half blurs.
  - **强化**：At 00:02.500, static close-up with micro push in on the young monkey squinting and reading the banner aloud, trailing off as the second half blurs. Only eyes and mouth move; face shape held invariant.

### E02-03
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, static shot, a static medium shot shows the young monkey sprinting up to block a wood-cutter carrying a yoke and bowing with hands clasped to ask the way.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, static breathing handheld medium shot shows the young monkey sprinting up to block a wood-cutter and bowing with hands clasped. His stop is a single clean brake; figure stays stable.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:02.500, static shot, a static close-up on the young monkey asking the way with courteous eagerness.
  - **强化**：At 00:02.500, static close-up with slow push in on the young monkey asking the way with courteous eagerness. Polite bow is small; fur and ears settle naturally.
- **Shot 3** 原文：At 00:05.400, static shot, a static medium shot on the wood-cutter who gestures toward the northern path and gives the way in three turns.
  - **强化**：At 00:05.400, slow lateral track over a medium frame on the wood-cutter who gestures toward the northern path in three turns. Gesture arm sweeps in one smooth arc; he remains one stable form.

### E02-04
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, static shot, a static close-up on the young monkey whose eyes light up as he presses the question.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, static breathing handheld close-up on the young monkey whose eyes light up as he presses the question. Only the glint in his eyes changes; face holds still.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:02.400, static shot, a static close-up on the wood-cutter who shakes his head gently and states the plain truth in a steady voice.
  - **强化**：At 00:02.400, static close-up with slow push in on the wood-cutter who shakes his head gently, stating the plain truth. Head shake measured and small; beard and yoke sway subtly.
- **Shot 3** 原文：At 00:07.500, static shot, a static medium shot shows the young monkey nodding, his grin settling into resolve as he turns toward the pointed way.
  - **强化**：At 00:07.500, gentle dolly-out to medium as the young monkey nods, grin settling into resolve as he turns toward the pointed way. Turn is paced; form stays coherent.

### E02-05
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, a static shot follows the young monkey climbing the stone steps higher, rounding a bend into lamplight and incense from a mountain gate.
  - **强化**：Following <Picture 1>, a static shot with slow tilt follows the young monkey climbing the stone steps higher, rounding a bend into lamplight and incense. Climb is steady; smoke and light waft while his form stays stable.
- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:07.500, static shot, a static medium shot peers into the hall where an old white-bearded Daoist master sits behind an incense table as the young monkey steps inside.
  - **强化**：At 00:07.500, slow push in over a medium frame peers into the hall where an old white-bearded Daoist master sits behind an incense table. Incense smoke rises gently; both figures remain immobile and sharp.

# E08

### E08-01

- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:05.000, static shot, a static close-up on the monk bending to study the golden strip, reading the weathered scripture with quiet thought <d>[Chinese] 石上贴着金牒，写的竟是古来经文……莫非要用在此处。</d>

### E08-02
- **Shot 1** 原文：Following &lt;Picture 1&gt;, static shot, a static close-up on the monkey head in the crevice, voice urgent and bright, asking if the monk has come to free him <d>[Chinese] 和尚，你是来放俺的么？</d>

- **Shot 2** 原文：At 00:02.400, static shot, a static close-up on the monk with palms joined, explaining that a golden strip awaits the destined hand to lift it <d>[Chinese] 贫僧奉旨西行取经，路径此山，见金牒言你待有缘人来揭。</d>

- **Shot 3** 原文：At 00:08.200, static shot, a static close-up on the eager monkey head straining forward, urging the monk to lift the strip after five hundred years <d>[Chinese] 俺在这底下等了五百年！快快帮俺揭了它！</d>

### E08-03
